/* TO DETECT BROWSER */

	window.onload = function()
    {
        checkBrowser();
    }
    function checkBrowser()
    {
        var ie = /MSIE (\d+)/.exec(navigator.userAgent);
        ie = ie? ie[1] : null;
		//alert(ie);
        if(ie && ie <= 9)
        {
            alert('Your Browser is out of date. To get best possible experience for our site please upgarde.');
			window.location="https://browser-update.org/update.html";
        }
    }

/* BROWSER TOKEN SAVE */
		function savebrowsertoken(token){
			var referral_url ="";
			if (document.referrer && document.referrer != ""){
				if(document.referrer.indexOf(window.location.host) === -1){ 
						//referral_save(document.referrer,window.location.href);
						referral_url =document.referrer;
				}
			}
			//alert(token);
			if(token!=''){
				var useragent = 'Mozilla/5.0 (Windows NT 10.0; Win64; x64) AppleWebKit/537.36 (KHTML, like Gecko) HeadlessChrome/77.0.3865.120 Safari/537.36';
				$.ajax({
					url:'https://www.casioindiashop.com/ajax-save-browser-token.php',
					data: {'token':token,'useragent':useragent,'referral_url':referral_url},
					type:'POST',
					success: function(results){
						
					}
				})
			}
		}
/* END OF BROWSER TOKEN SAVE */

$(document).ready(function(){
	
			
			/* MAIN SEARCH */
			$(document).on('click','.headerseachnew',function(e){
					var keyword = $(this).attr('rel');
					var catid='';
					//window.location='https://www.casioindiashop.com/product-listing.php?keyword='+keyword;
						 $.ajax({
							url: 'https://www.casioindiashop.com/check-keyword.php',
							type: 'POST',
							data: {'keyword':keyword},
							success: function(result){
								$("#loading-new").css('display','none');
								var res=$.parseJSON(result);
								//alert(res.result);
									if(res.result=="success"){
										if(res.catid!=null){
										window.location='https://www.casioindiashop.com/product-listing.php?catid='+res.catid;		
										} else {
										window.location='https://www.casioindiashop.com/search.php?keyword='+keyword;
										}
									}else{
									if(catid!=''){
										window.location='https://www.casioindiashop.com/product-listing.php?catid='+catid+'&keyword='+keyword;
										}else{
										window.location='https://www.casioindiashop.com/search.php?keyword='+keyword;	
										}	
									}
							}
						}) 
					
			}); 



	function showMenu(prmBut,prmMenu){

		$(prmBut).hover(function(){

			$(prmMenu).stop(true, true).fadeIn("200");

			$(this).addClass("active");

		}, function(){

			$(prmMenu).hide();

			$(this).removeClass("active");

		});

		$(prmMenu).hover(function(){

			$(this).show();

			$(prmBut).addClass("active");

		}, function(){

			$(this).fadeOut("fast");

			$(prmBut).removeClass("active");

		});

		}

		showMenu(".hdd1","#m1");

		showMenu(".hdd2","#m2");

		showMenu(".hdd3","#m3");

		showMenu(".hdd4","#m4");

		showMenu(".hdd5","#m5");

		showMenu(".hdd6","#m6");

		showMenu(".hdd7","#m7");

		

		

		$(window).scroll(function() {

		scrollPos = $(window).scrollTop();

		if (scrollPos > 350){

			$(".top").fadeIn(500);

		} else{

			$(".top").fadeOut(500);

		}

		

		if (scrollPos > 27){

				$("header > nav").css({"display":"block"});

				$("header").css({"position":"fixed","top":"0px"});
				
				$("header .headmid").css({"height":"84px"});

				$("header .headmid .logo").css({"margin-top":"20px"});

				$("header .headmid .searchbox").css({"top":"20px"});

				$("header .headmid .login").css({"top":"24px"});

				$(".hbanner, .pagebanner, .breadcrumb").css({"margin-top":"0px"});

				$(".thanks").css({"margin-top":"40px"});

				

			} else{

				$("header > nav").css({"display":"block"});

				$("header").css({"position":"relative","top":"0"});

				$("header .headmid").css({"height":"84px"});

				$("header .headmid .logo").css({"margin-top":"20px"});

				$("header .headmid .searchbox").css({"top":"20px"});

				$("header .headmid .login").css({"top":"24px"});

				$(".hbanner, .pagebanner, .breadcrumb").css({"margin-top":"0px"});

				$(".thanks").css({"margin-top":"40px"});

			}

		});

		

		$( document).on('click',".loginbut",function() {

			$(".overlayL").fadeIn("fast");

			$(".loginpop").fadeIn("fast");

		});

		$( document).on('click',".loginclose",function() {

			$(".overlayL").fadeOut("fast");

			$(".loginpop").fadeOut("fast");

		$(document).on('click',".notifyemail",function(){

			$(".forgotdiv").fadeOut("fast");

		});

		

		$( document).on('click',".forgotbut",function() {

			$(".forgotdiv-in").fadeIn("fast");

		});

		$( document).on('click',".forgotclose-in",function() {

			$(".forgotdiv-in").fadeOut("fast");

		});

		

		$(".mleftw a:first-child, .mleftc a:first-child, .mlefte a:first-child").addClass("active");

		$(".mrightw div").hide();

		$( document).on('click',".findbut",function() {

			$(".searchbox").slideToggle("slow");

		$( document).on('click','.closemmenu',function() {

			$('.mobmenucont').stop(true, true).animate({left:'-85%'}, 200, 'easeInQuad');

		$(".leftpanel").slideToggle("slow");

		});

		

		$( document).on('click',".notification",function() {
			$(".notidiv").fadeIn("fast");
			$(".notioverlay").fadeIn("fast");
			$(".ntfn").css('display', "block");
			$(".ntfn").css('zIndex', 999999);
		});
		$( document).on('click',".ntfn",function() {
			$(".notidiv").fadeOut("fast");
			$(".notioverlay").fadeOut("fast");
			$(".ntfn").css('display', "none");
		});	
		$( document).on('click',".notificationclose",function() {
			$(".notidiv").fadeOut("fast");
			$(".notioverlay").fadeOut("fast");
			$(".ntfn").css('display', "none");
		});

		

		$( document).on('click',".techbut",function() {

			$(".tlinks").slideToggle("slow");

			$(".techbut i").toggleClass("iactive");

			$(".toggleoverlaybut").slideToggle("fast");

		});

			

		$(window).resize(function(){

			resizeScreen();	

		});

		

		function resizeScreen(){

			screenWidth = $(window).width();

			screenHeight = $(window).height();	

			if(screenWidth > 1003 ){

				$(".leftpanel").addClass("displayy");

			}else if(screenWidth < 1003){

				$(".leftpanel").removeClass("displayy");

			}

			}	

		

});

<script>
function validate_email(email){	
			var filter = /^([\w-\.]+)@((\[[0-9]{1,3}\.[0-9]{1,3}\.[0-9]{1,3}\.)|(([\w-]+\.)+))([a-zA-Z]{2,4}|[0-9]{1,3})(\]?)$/;
				if (filter.test(email)) {
					return true;
				}else{
					return false;
				}
		}

function subscribed_user(){
	var subscriber_email = $('#subscriber_email').val();
	if(subscriber_email!=''){
		var filter = /^([\w-\.]+)@((\[[0-9]{1,3}\.[0-9]{1,3}\.[0-9]{1,3}\.)|(([\w-]+\.)+))([a-zA-Z]{2,4}|[0-9]{1,3})(\]?)$/;
		if (filter.test(subscriber_email)) {
				$.ajax({
					url: 'https://www.casioindiashop.com/ajax_subscribe_users.php',
					type: 'POST',
					data:{'subscriber_email':  window.btoa(subscriber_email)},
					success: function(result){
						var res = $.parseJSON(result);
						if(res.result=='success'){
							$('#subscriber_email').val("");
						$('.subscribed_user').animate({bottom: "40px"}, 400);
						setTimeout(function(){$('.subscribed_user').animate({bottom: "-95px"},400);},6000);
						}else{
							if(res.data==2){
								$('#subscriber_email').val("");
							$('.alreadysubscribed_user').animate({bottom: "40px"}, 400);
							setTimeout(function(){$('.alreadysubscribed_user').animate({bottom: "-95px"},400);},6000);	
							}
						}
					}
				})
		}else{
			$('#subscriber_email').css('background', '#fff5a0');
		$('#subscriber_email').focus();
		}
	}else{
		$('#subscriber_email').css('background', '#fff5a0');
		$('#subscriber_email').focus();
	}
}

/* COMBO PRODUCT ADDE TO CART FUNCTION */
	function add_combo_cart(combosku){
		var skus = new Array();
		skus = combosku.split("||"); 
		var i=0;
		for(i=0;i<skus.length;i++){
		var sku = skus[i];
		var value = $('.sessionspanCartTotalQTY').val();
				       
			$.ajax({
			type: "post",
			url:"https://www.casioindiashop.com/ajax_add_to_cart.php",//url of the page where php,mysql code
			data:{"sku":sku},
				success: function(result){
					var res= jQuery.parseJSON(result);
					if(res.result=="success"){
						
						/********************* START ****************/
							$.ajax({
								type: "post",
								url:"https://www.casioindiashop.com/ajax-get-product-detail.php",//url of the page where php,mysql code
								data:{"sku":sku},
									success: function(results){
									var res = $.parseJSON(results);
										if(res.result=='success'){
											var productObj = res.response;
											dataLayer.push({
													'event': 'addToCart',
													'ecommerce': {
														'currencyCode': 'INR',
														'add': {
															'products': [{
															  'name': productObj.name,                      // Name or ID is required.
															  'id': productObj.id,
															  'price': productObj.price,
															  'brand': productObj.brand,
															  'category': productObj.cat,
															  'variant': '',
															  'quantity': 1
															 }]
														}
													}
											});
										}
										
									}
							});
							/********************* END ****************/
							
						if(i==2){
							//$('.additemintocart').animate({bottom: "100px"}, 400);
							window.location="https://www.casioindiashop.com/checkout.php";
						}
					$('.spanCartTotalQTY').val(res.totalitem);
					$('.spanCartTotalQTY').html('<span class="mobilehidebag">Items in <br>Your Bag</span><i class="borderradius">'+res.totalitem+'</i>');
					setTimeout(function(){$('.additemintocart').animate({bottom: "-95px"},400);},6000);
					} else {
						$('.alreadycart').animate({bottom: "40px"}, 400);
						setTimeout(function(){$('.alreadycart').animate({bottom: "-95px"},400);},6000);
					}
				}
			});
				}
	}

function notification_view(){

		$.ajax({
			url: 'https://www.casioindiashop.com/ajax_notification_view.php',
			type: 'POST',
			data: {'memberid':memberid},
			success: function(results){
				
			}
			
		})
	}
}
/* CHECK INPUT IS NUMERIC OR NOT */
	 function isNumberKey(evt)
      {
         var charCode = (evt.which) ? evt.which : event.keyCode
         if (charCode > 31 && (charCode < 48 || charCode > 57))
            return false;

         return true;
      }
	  

	  
	  
/* ON ENTER HEADER SEARCH BAR WORKS */
function header_search(keyword){
	if(keyword!=''){
		var keyword = keyword;
	}
	if(keyword==''){
		var keyword = $('.searchkeyword').val();
	}
	if(keyword!=''){
	$("#loading-new").css('display','block');
	
	var catid='';
	//window.location='https://www.casioindiashop.com/product-listing.php?keyword='+keyword;
	$.ajax({
				url: 'https://www.casioindiashop.com/check-keyword.php',
				type: 'POST',
				data: {'keyword':keyword},
				success: function(result){
					$("#loading-new").css('display','none');
					var res=$.parseJSON(result);
					//alert(res.result);
						if(res.result=="success"){
							if(res.catid!=null){
							window.location='https://www.casioindiashop.com/product-listing.php?catid='+res.catid;		
							} else {
							window.location='https://www.casioindiashop.com/product-listing.php?keyword='+keyword;
							}
						}else{
						if(catid!=''){
							window.location='https://www.casioindiashop.com/product-listing.php?catid='+catid+'&&keyword='+keyword;
							}else{
							window.location='https://www.casioindiashop.com/product-listing.php?keyword='+keyword;	
							}	
						}
				}
			}) 
	}	
}
/* ADD NEW REVIEW */
function addnewreview(){
			var review_title = $('#review_title').val();
			var rating = $('#rating_simple').val();
			var review_text = $('#review_text').val();
			var product_sku = $('#sku_for_review').val();
			var memberid = '';
			if(memberid==''){
				var memberid = '';
			}
			$('.review_label').show();
			$("#loading-new").css('display','block');
			$.ajax({
				type: "post",
				url:"https://www.casioindiashop.com/inc_add_reviews.php",//url of the page where php,mysql code
				data:{"product_sku":product_sku,"review_title":review_title,"rating":rating,"review_text":review_text,"memberid":memberid},
					success: function(result){
						var res = $.parseJSON(result);
						console.log(res);
						if(res.result=="success"){
							$("#loading-new").css('display','none');
						$('.review_label').html('<span class="successmsg">'+res.message+'</span>');
						$('.reviewproduct').animate({bottom: "40px"}, 400);
						setTimeout(function(){$('.reviewproduct').animate({bottom: "-95px"},400);},6000);
						$('#review_title').val("");
						$('#rating_simple').val("");
						$('#review_text').val("");
						if(res.total_reviews>0){
						$('#total_reviews').html(res.total_reviews+' Review(s)');
						}else{
						$('#total_reviews').html('No Review');	
						}
						//setTimeout(function(e){$('.review_label').hide();},3000);
						$('.wtreview').fadeOut("fast");
						$('.overlay-rev').hide();
						}else{
							$("#loading-new").css('display','none');
							if(res.result=="failure"){
								if(res.message!="Mandatory fields are missing."){
								$('.alreadyreviewproduct').animate({bottom: "40px"}, 400);
								setTimeout(function(){$('.alreadyreviewproduct').animate({bottom: "-95px"},400);},6000);
								$('.review_label').html('<span class="failuremsg">'+res.message+'</span>');
								$('.wtreview').fadeOut("fast");
								$('.overlay-rev').hide();
								}else{
								$('.review_label').html('<span class="failuremsg">'+res.message+'</span>');	
								}
							}
						}
					}
			})
	}

		  if (el.value.length != 6) {
			  $(".cod_label").show();
			$(".cod_label").html('<span class="failuremsg">Please enter 6 digits pincode</span>');
		  } else {

	}

/*WHEN PRODUCT OUT OF STOCK NOTIFICATION  */
function notifyproduct(){
	var sEmail = $('#notifyemail').val();
	var product_sku = $('#notifyemail').attr('rel');
	$('.notify_label').show();
	$('.notify_label').html("");
    var filter = /^([\w-\.]+)@((\[[0-9]{1,3}\.[0-9]{1,3}\.[0-9]{1,3}\.)|(([\w-]+\.)+))([a-zA-Z]{2,4}|[0-9]{1,3})(\]?)$/;
    if (filter.test(sEmail)) {
		setTimeout(function(e){$('.notify_label').hide();});
		$("#loading-new").css('display','block');
    $.ajax({
			type: 'POST',
			url: 'https://www.casioindiashop.com/ajax-product-notify.php',
			data: {'notificationemail':sEmail,'product_sku':product_sku},
			success: function(result){
				$("#loading-new").css('display','none');
				var res = $.parseJSON(result);
					if(res.result=="success"){
					$('.notifyemailmsg').animate({bottom: "40px"}, 400);
					$('#notifyemail').val("");	
					setTimeout(function(){$('.notifyemailmsg').animate({bottom: "-95px"},400);},6000);
					}
			}
		});
    }
    else {
        $('.notify_label').html('<span class="failuremsg" style="margin-top:2px">Please specify a valid Email Address</span>');
	}
}

	/* ADD TO CART FUNCTION */
	$(document).on('click','.shopping',function(e){
		e.preventDefault();
		var sku = $(this).attr('rel');
		var value = $('.sessionspanCartTotalQTY').val();
				    $("#loading-new").css('display','block');
			$.ajax({
			type: "post",
			url:"https://www.casioindiashop.com/ajax_add_to_cart.php",//url of the page where php,mysql code
			data:{"sku":sku},
				success: function(result){
					var res= jQuery.parseJSON(result);
					if(res.result=="success"){
						$("#loading-new").css('display','none');
						
						/********************* START ****************/
							$.ajax({
								type: "post",
								url:"https://www.casioindiashop.com/ajax-get-product-detail.php",//url of the page where php,mysql code
								data:{"sku":sku},
									success: function(results){
									var res = $.parseJSON(results);
										if(res.result=='success'){
											var productObj = res.response;
											dataLayer.push({
													'event': 'addToCart',
													'ecommerce': {
														'currencyCode': 'INR',
														'add': {
															'products': [{
															  'name': productObj.name,                      // Name or ID is required.
															  'id': productObj.id,
															  'price': productObj.price,
															  'brand': productObj.brand,
															  'category': productObj.cat,
															  'variant': '',
															  'quantity': 1
															 }]
														}
													}
											});
										}
										
									}
							});
							/********************* END ****************/
							
					$('.additemintocart').animate({bottom: "100px"}, 400);
					$('.spanCartTotalQTY').val(res.totalitem);
					$('.spanCartTotalQTY').html('<span class="mobilehidebag">Items in <br>Your Bag</span><i class="borderradius">'+res.totalitem+'</i>');
					setTimeout(function(){$('.additemintocart').animate({bottom: "-95px"},400);},6000);
					} else {
						$("#loading-new").css('display','none');
						$('.alreadycart').animate({bottom: "100px"}, 400);
						setTimeout(function(){$('.alreadycart').animate({bottom: "-95px"},400);},6000);
					}
				}
			});
				
	
	});

	function checkPasswordMatch() {
		$("#registererrmsg").show();
		$("#registererrmsg").html("");
				var password = $(".txtpassword").val();
				var confirmPassword = $(".txtconfirmpassword").val();
				if(password !=''){
					if (password != confirmPassword){
						//$("#registererrmsg").show();
						$("#registererrmsg").html('<span class="failuremsg">Passwords do not match!</span>');
					    setTimeout(function(){$("#registererrmsg").hide();},2000);
					}else{
						$("#registererrmsg").html("");
					}
				}
     }
	 
	function registercheckLength(el) {
		  if (el.value.length != 10) {
			  $("#registererrmsg").show();
			$("#registererrmsg").html('<span class="failuremsg">Please enter 10 digits mobile number</span>');
		  } else {
			  $("#registererrmsg").html("");
		  }
	}
	function registercheckpasswordLength(el) {
		  if (el.value.length < 6) {
			  $("#registererrmsg").show();
			$("#registererrmsg").html('<span class="failuremsg">Password should be minimum 6 characters</span>');
		  } else {
			  $("#registererrmsg").html("");
		  }
	}
	
	/* MEMBER LOGIN FUNCTION */
	
	function login_user() {
		  $("#errmsg").show();
		  $("#errmsg").html("");
		  if($('#loginemail').val() == '') {
			  $('#loginemail').css('border-color', 'red');
		  } else {
			$('#loginemail').css('border-color', '');
		  } 
		  
		  if($('#loginpassword').val() == '') {
			  $('#loginpassword').css('border-color', 'red');
		  } else {
			$('#loginpassword').css('border-color', '');
		  }
           var x=$('#loginemail').val();
			var regex=/^[a-zA-Z]+$/;
			if (!x.match(regex))
			{
			var check = true;
			}else{
			var check = validate_email($('#loginemail').val());	
			}		  
		  if($('#loginemail').val() != '' && $('#loginpassword').val() != '') { 
			if(check==true){	
			var key='2836dbcd4310d3527298229b58cd696c';
			var inputemail = $('#loginemail').val();
			var email = inputemail.toLowerCase();
			var password = $('#loginpassword').val();
			
			var md5email=MD5(email);
			email=md5email
	 
			var mdpassword=MD5(password);		
			var mdpass1=(mdpassword);  
			var mdpass2=MD5(mdpass1+key); 
			password=mdpass2;
			
			var sentdata= {'email':email,'password':password};
			var requestdata = JSON.stringify(sentdata);
			$("#loading-new").css('display','block');	
			$.ajax({
					type: "POST",
					url: "https://www.casioindiashop.com/inc_ajax_login.php",
					data: {'requestdata':encodeURIComponent(requestdata),'requesttype':'login'},   
					success: function (result) {
						var res= jQuery.parseJSON(result);
						console.log(res);
						$('.error_response').html('');
						if(res.result=="failure")
						{														
							$('#loading-new').css('display','none');							
							if(res.message)
							{
								$('.error_response').html('<span>'+res.message+'</span>');
							}
						}
						if(res.result=="success")
						{													
							$('#loading-new').css('display','none');							
							//alert(res.message);
							if(res.data==1){
								$('#verifymobile').val(res.mobile);
								$('.loginpop').hide();
								$('#loginemail').val("");
								$('#loginpassword').val("");
								 /* document.getElementById("member_register").reset(); */
								$('.otppop').show();
							}else{
								$('#loginemail').val("");
								$('#loginpassword').val("");
							/* document.getElementById("member_register").reset(); */
							$("#errmsg").html('<span class="successmsg">'+res.message+'</span>');
							//setTimeout(function(){$("#errmsg").hide();},2000);
							location.reload();
							}
						}
						else
						{
							$("#loading-new").css('display','none');
							if(res.message)
							{
							$("#errmsg").html('<span class="failuremsg">'+res.message+'</span>');
							//setTimeout(function(){$("#errmsg").hide();},4000);
							}
						}
					}
				})
			}else{
				$("#errmsg").html('<span class="failuremsg">Please enter a valid email address.</span>');
			}
			}
		}
	
	
	/* MEMBER REGISTER FUNCTION */
	
	function register_user() {
		  
		  $("#registererrmsg").show();
		  $("#registererrmsg").html("");
		  $('#verifymobile').val("");
		  if ($('#txtname').val() == '') {
				$('#txtname').css('border-color', 'red');
		  } else {
			$('#txtname').css('border-color', '');
		  } 
		  
		  if($('#txtemail').val() == '') {
			  $('#txtemail').css('border-color', 'red');
		  } else {
			$('#txtemail').css('border-color', '');
		  } 
		  var check = validate_email($('#txtemail').val());
		  if($('#mobile').val() == '') {
			  $('#mobile').css('border-color', 'red');
		  } else {
			$('#mobile').css('border-color', '');
		  } 
		  
		  if($('#password').val() == '') {
			  $('#password').css('border-color', 'red');
		  } else {
			$('#password').css('border-color', '');
		  }  
		  
		  if($('#confirmpassword').val() == '') {
			  $('#confirmpassword').css('border-color', 'red');
		  } else {
			$('#confirmpassword').css('border-color', '');
		  } 
		  
		 	 
		  if($("input[type='checkbox']:checked").val() == 'undefined'){
			 $("#registererrmsg").html('<span class="failuremsg">Please accept Terms and Conditions to continue</span>') 
		  }
		  
		  
		  
		  if($('.txtpassword').val() === $('.txtconfirmpassword').val()) {
			} 
		  
		  if($('#txtname').val() != '' && $('#txtemail').val() != '' && $('#mobile').val() != '' ) { 
				if(check==true){
					var name = btoa($('#txtname').val());
					var email = btoa($('#txtemail').val());
					var mobile = btoa($('#mobile').val());
					var password = btoa($('#password').val());
					var confirmpassword = btoa($('#confirmpassword').val());
					var confirmpassword = btoa($('#confirmpassword').val());
					var gender = $('input[name="gender"]:checked').val();
					var sentdata= {'name':name,'email':email,'mobile':mobile,'password':password,'confirmpassword':confirmpassword,'gender':gender};
					var requestdata = JSON.stringify(sentdata);
					$("#loading-new").css('display','block');
				   $.ajax({
						type: "POST",
						url: "https://www.casioindiashop.com/inc_ajax_register.php",
						data: {'requestdata':encodeURIComponent(requestdata),'requesttype':'register'},   
						success: function (result) {
							var res= jQuery.parseJSON(result);
							console.log(res);
							$('.error_response').html('');
							if(res.result=="failure")
							{
								$("#loading-new").css('display','none');
								if(res.message)
								{
									$('.error_response').html('<span>'+res.message+'</span>');
								}
							}
							if(res.result=="success")
							{
								$("#loading-new").css('display','none');
								$('#txtname').val("");
								$('#txtemail').val("");
								$('#mobile').val("");
								$('#password').val("");
								$('#confirmpassword').val("");
								//document.getElementById("member_register").reset();
								$("#registererrmsg").html('<span class="successmsg">'+res.message+'</span>');
								//setTimeout(function(){$("#registererrmsg").hide();},2000);
								$('.signuppop').hide();
								$('#verifymobile').val(res.mobile);
								//$('.otppop').show();
								$('#wlcmmsg').html("Welcome! "+res.username);
								$('.welcomepop').show();
							}
							else
							{
								$("#loading-new").css('display','none');
								if(res.message)
								{
								$("#registererrmsg").html('<span class="failuremsg">'+res.message+'</span>');
								//setTimeout(function(){$("#registererrmsg").hide();},6000);
								}
							}
						}
					})
				}else{
					$("#registererrmsg").html('<span class="failuremsg">Please enter a valid email address.</span>');
				}
			}
        }

</script><style>
